### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

Taiwan-standard 麼 participates in lexical mappings to 么 and Mainland 麽; the official note preserves 麽 for mó, so a context-free target would be misleading.

U+9EBC

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

The official table places 妳 under both 奶 and 你, so no context-free single mapping exists.

U+59B3

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

夥 maps to 伙 in applicable uses but remains 夥 when it means many.

U+5925

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

干 is used for the simplified dry sense, while 乾 remains normative for qián uses such as 乾坤 and 乾隆.

U+4E7E

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

徵 maps to 征 generally but remains 徵 for the musical-tone name zhǐ.

U+5FB5

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

於 is itself a normative PRC character, while exact dictionary entries also map prepositional uses to 于; character-only context cannot select one.

U+65BC

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

昇 is normally normalized to 升 but remains permitted in personal names.

U+6607

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

沈 is normative for surname and Shenyang uses but is also listed as a variant of 沉 for other senses.

U+6C88

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

瞭 maps to 了 in applicable uses but remains 瞭 when read liào, including 瞭望 and 瞭哨.

U+77AD

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

線 maps to 线 generally but to 缐 when used as a surname.

U+7DDA

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

著 and 着 are separate normative characters and exact lexical mappings depend on grammatical or lexical use.

U+8457

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

藉 maps to 借 in applicable uses but remains 藉 for jí and for the consolation or matting senses.

U+85C9

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

蘋 maps to 苹 generally but to 𬞟 for the plant-name use identified by the official note.

U+860B

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

覆 is itself normative and is no longer a general traditional form of 复, but exact dictionary entries retain lexical 复 mappings; context is required.

U+8986

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

鍾 maps to 钟 generally but may map to 锺 when used as a surname.

U+937E

### `character.simplified` — 1 record(s)

阪 is normalized to 坂 in applicable uses but remains permitted in place names such as 大阪.
